# Graph Report - TMS AHORA SI  (2026-11-04)

## Corpus Check
- 2621 files · ~2,618,442 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 87 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: .sha256 20, .toml 16, (none) 10)

## Summary
- 28550 nodes · 58281 edges · 1255 communities (989 shown, 266 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 2326 edges (avg confidence: 0.92)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `4d1acf53`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
